   Nan take ta fashe da kuka cikin kuka ta sanar da shi gaskiya mr Kenneth adopted dinshi ya yi a gidan marayu kasancewar bashi haihuwa, amma ya sani har gobe tana kallonshi a matsayin d'an su tana kuma taya shi murna gane biological parents dinshi da ya yi.
   Tsabar farin ciki da kuma suna so su yi surprising family su yasa washe gari suka dawo Nigeria tare da Joel.
   Lokaci da Umma maryama tayi arba da d'anta da ta fidda ran gani saida ta suma aka yayyafa mata ruwa ta farfad'o, Daddy ranar kamar zai yi hauka.
    *AFTER THREE MONTHS*

    Burin Daddy ya cika iyalinshi sun kammala kuma suna cikin farin ciki.
